## 🔁 Overview: Agent Loop Wiring Plan

---

You’ll wire your agents together inside a **single Pipedream workflow**, running every 5 minutes (or whatever interval you choose).

**Step Order (Workflow Chain):**

1. `Market Data Aggregator`
2. `Market Regime Classifier`
3. `News & Sentiment Agent`
4. `Stock Pattern Recognition Agent`
5. `Trading Decision Agent`
6. `Trading Risk Gate Validator`
7. `Trading Execution Engine`
8. `Position Babysitter Risk Management Agent`

Each one **takes the previous step’s output** as its input.

---

## 🛠️ Step-by-Step Wiring Instructions

### 🧩 1. Create a New Workflow
- Go to Pipedream
- Click ➕ **New Workflow**
- Choose **"Scheduled" trigger** → every 5 mins

---

### ⚙️ 2. Add Your First Step: Market Data Aggregator
- Click **+** below the trigger
- Search for or choose **“Run another workflow”**
- Pick your **Market Data Aggregator** agent
- Name the step: `market_data`

---

### ➡️ 3. Add Market Regime Classifier
- Add a new step: **Run another workflow**
- Select: **Market Regime Classifier**
- Under “**Pass Input**”:
  - Enable toggle
  - Add this key-value pair:
    ```json
    {
      "market_data": steps.market_data.$return_value
    }
    ```
- Name this step: `regime_class`

---

### ➕ 4. News & Sentiment Agent
- New step → Run another workflow → Select News & Sentiment Agent
- Input:
  ```json
  {
    "market_data": steps.market_data.$return_value,
    "regime": steps.regime_class.$return_value
  }
  ```
- Name: `news_sentiment`

---

### 🧠 5. Stock Pattern Recognition Agent
- Add → Run another workflow → Select `Stock Pattern Recognition Agent`
- Input:
  ```json
  {
    "market_data": steps.market_data.$return_value,
    "sentiment": steps.news_sentiment.$return_value
  }
  ```
- Name: `pattern_recognition`

---

### 💡 6. Trading Decision Agent
- Add → Run another workflow → Select `Trading Decision Agent`
- Input:
  ```json
  {
    "pattern_data": steps.pattern_recognition.$return_value,
    "regime": steps.regime_class.$return_value,
    "sentiment": steps.news_sentiment.$return_value
  }
  ```
- Name: `trade_decision`

---

### 🛑 7. Trading Risk Gate Validator
- Add → Run another workflow → Select `Trading Risk Gate Validator`
- Input:
  ```json
  {
    "trade": steps.trade_decision.$return_value
  }
  ```
- Name: `risk_validator`

---

### 🛒 8. Trading Execution Engine
- Add → Run another workflow → Select `Trading Execution Engine`
- Input:
  ```json
  {
    "validated_trade": steps.risk_validator.$return_value
  }
  ```
- Name: `execution_engine`

---

### 👀 9. Babysitter Agent
- Final step → Run another workflow → Select `Position Babysitter Risk Management Agent`
- Input:
  ```json
  {
    "position": steps.execution_engine.$return_value.position
  }
  ```
- Name: `babysitter`

---

- Hit **Test** (on any step) or let it run naturally on the schedule.

To **run another workflow (to wire your loop)**, Pipedream doesn’t label it exactly like that — but here's what to do:

---

### ✅ **Step-by-Step: Trigger Another Workflow (Loop or Chain Workflows)**

#### 🧩 Option 1: Use HTTP / Webhook Action

1. In the **Add action** panel (from your screenshot), scroll to or search for:

---

**`HTTP / Webhook → Make HTTP Request`**
2. Select it.
3. In the setup panel:
   - **URL**: paste the **Webhook URL** of the workflow you want to trigger (you get this by opening the other workflow and copying its "HTTP Trigger" URL).
   - **Method**: `POST`
   - **Body**: Use `JSON` format to pass data (e.g. trade decisions, tickers, etc.).
     ```json
     {
       "symbol": "AAPL",
       "action": "BUY",
       "price": 217.42
     }
     ```

---

#### 🔁 Option 2: Call Your Own Workflow Using "My Actions"

If the next workflow you want to run is **already created in the same project**, you can:

1. Click **"My Actions"** from the menu in the left of your screenshot.
2. If your other workflows are listed there, click it to reuse it as a module.
3. If it’s not listed, it’s likely because:
   - It doesn’t have an HTTP/Webhook trigger set up.
   - Or it’s not yet **deployed**.

---

### 🛠 Next Step If “N” Workflow (undeployed) Needs Wiring

If the “N” (undeployed) workflow is the one you’re trying to **loop into the sequence**, do this:

1. Open that workflow in a new tab.
2. Make sure it starts with a **Webhook trigger** (`HTTP Trigger` block).
3. Click **Deploy**.
4. Copy the webhook URL from the top.
5. Return to your current workflow.
6. Add **HTTP / Webhook > Make Request**, and paste that URL there.

---

### ⚠️ Bonus Tips

- **Time to Live (TTL)** is for data store expiration. If you’re saving positions/orders in a `Data Store`, TTL auto-deletes them after the set time.
- To avoid `NOOP`, make sure your upstream step (e.g. Trading Decision Agent) sends the proper structure and valid values.

---

## 🧠 What’s Actually Happening

---

You're in the **workflow builder**, and you want one agent (a workflow) to **trigger another**. Since they're all part of the same project, the best approach is to **use an HTTP Trigger** to connect them. But you won’t find something labeled like "Run My Workflow" — it’s more like wiring pipes with URLs.

---

## ✅ Step-by-Step: Connect Your Agents Together

### 🔹 Step 1: Make sure the target agent (workflow) has a Webhook Trigger
Let’s say you want agent `N` to be called from another agent.

1. Go to that agent's workflow (in another tab).
2. Make sure the first step is an **HTTP / Webhook → HTTP Trigger** (it shows a lightning bolt ⚡).
3. Click on it and copy the **HTTP endpoint URL** (it’s at the top right of the step or workflow).
4. Hit **Deploy** to make sure it’s live.

---

### 🔹 Step 2: In your main workflow, call the agent via HTTP

1. Go back to the workflow you’re building from (the "caller" workflow).
2. Click `+` to add a new step.
3. Choose:
   - `HTTP / Webhook → Make HTTP Request`
4. Paste the **URL you copied** in the `URL` field.
5. Set method to `POST`
6. Under body, choose JSON and send whatever payload you want:
   ```json
   {
     "ticker": "AAPL",
     "action": "BUY"
   }
   ```

---

### ❓ Why you don’t see “My Actions”

That section **only shows “custom modules”** you’ve manually published as actions. But Pipedream doesn’t auto-expose your workflows there — it expects you to trigger them via **HTTP** (webhook) or **schedule**, not from a dropdown.

---

### ✅ Optional: Send Data Between Agents

If you want your trading decision agent to send its output directly into the execution or babysitter agents:

- Just pass the output from `steps.tradingDecisionAgent.$return_value` into your `HTTP Request` body when calling the next agent.
- Example:
   ```js
   const trade = steps.tradingDecisionAgent.$return_value;
   return {
     ticker: trade.ticker,
     action: trade.action,
     entry_price: trade.entry_price
   };
   ```

---

You're currently looking at HTTP / Webhook actions in Pipedream, but to **wire one of your existing agents (workflows) to another**, what you *actually* want is to:

---

### ✅ **Trigger Another Workflow Within the Same Project**
Here’s how to do that:

#### **Step 1: Make sure your target workflow has a trigger**
- The workflow you want to run (the target) must be set to start with **an HTTP/Webhook trigger**.
- Open that agent (like the one labeled `N` or any other you want to trigger) → edit it → **set its first step to a Webhook trigger** and deploy it.

#### **Step 2: Copy the Webhook URL**
- Once it's deployed, it will generate a **unique webhook URL** (something like `https://eo1234.m.pipedream.net`).
- You'll use that in the next step.

#### **Step 3: Back in your current workflow**, in the action where you're trying to **“wire the loop”**:
1. Select **HTTP / Webhook → Send POST Request**.
2. In the `URL` field, paste the **webhook URL** you copied from Step 2.
3. Set `Content-Type` to `application/json`.
4. In the `Request Body`, enter the data you want to send. For example:

```json
{
  "ticker": "AAPL",
  "entry_price": 217.42,
  "stop_loss": 214.0,
  "take_profits": [222.0, 227.0],
  "action": "BUY"
}
```

Or if you're pulling from previous steps:

```json
{
  "ticker": "{{steps.previous_step_name.ticker}}",
  "entry_price": "{{steps.previous_step_name.entry_price}}",
  "stop_loss": "{{steps.previous_step_name.stop_loss}}",
  "take_profits": "{{steps.previous_step_name.take_profits}}",
  "action": "{{steps.previous_step_name.action}}"
}
```
